Now the fundamental theorem. Let me define env_sem:
```coq
Definition env_sem (Gamma : context) (rho : env) : Prop :=
  (forall x s, env_lookup x rho = Some s -> locally_closed s) /\
  (forall x T, Gamma x = Some T -> exists s, env_lookup x rho = Some s /\ LR T s).
```

Lemma env_sem_cons:
```coq
Lemma env_sem_cons : forall Gamma rho x T u,
  env_sem Gamma rho ->
  LR T u ->
  env_sem (update Gamma x T) ((x, u) :: rho).
Proof.
  intros Gamma rho x T u [Hlc Hty] HRu. split.
  - intros y s Hs. simpl in Hs. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst. injection Hs as ->. apply LR_lc with (T := T); auto.
    + apply Hlc with y; auto.
  - intros y T' Hy. simpl. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E; subst. 
      (* update Gamma x T x = Some T1'? *) unfold update in Hy. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl in Hy. injection Hy as ->.
      exists u. split; auto.
    + apply Nat.eqb_neq in E. exists... from Hty y T' with Gamma y = Some T'.
      unfold update in Hy. rewrite E in Hy. destruct (Hty y T' Hy) as [s [Hs HLR]]. exists s; split; auto.
Qed.
```
Careful with `update Gamma x T y = if Nat.eqb x y then Some T else Gamma y` — note the update function uses `if Nat.eqb x y`, with x the update variable and y the query. Let me re-read: 
```
Definition update (Gamma : context) (x : atom) (T : ty) : context :=
  fun y => if Nat.eqb x y then Some T else Gamma y.
```
So `update Gamma x T y = if Nat.eqb x y then Some T else Gamma y`. Since Nat.eqb is symmetric, `Nat.eqb x y = Nat.eqb y x` ✓ (there's `Nat.eqb_sym` or we can use `Nat.eqb_eq`/`Nat.eqb_neq` which are symmetric: `Nat.eqb x y = true ↔ x = y` ✓).

Let me be careful in the proof. I'll handle it explicitly.

```coq
Lemma subst_fvar : forall rho x s, env_lookup x rho = Some s -> subst rho (tm_fvar x) = s.
Proof. intros; simpl. rewrite H. reflexivity. Qed.

Lemma subst_nil : forall t, subst [] t = t.
Proof. induction t; simpl; auto. 
  - (* fvar *) simpl. reflexivity. 
  - (* app *) rewrite IHt1, IHt2; auto.
  - (* abs *) rewrite IHt; auto.
Qed.
```

Freshness and env_atoms for the substitution-opening lemma:
```coq
Lemma lookup_cons_eq : forall x u rho, env_lookup x ((x,u)::rho) = Some u.
Proof. intros. simpl. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. reflexivity. Qed.

Lemma lookup_cons_neq : forall x y u rho, x <> y -> env_lookup x ((y,u)::rho) = env_lookup x rho.
Proof. intros. simpl. destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:E. apply Nat.eqb_eq in E; contradiction. reflexivity. Qed.
```

The main substitution-opening lemma:
```coq
Lemma subst_open_rec : forall rho x u k t,
  ~ In x (fv_list t) ->
  ~ In x (env_atoms rho) ->
  subst ((x,u)::rho) (open_rec k (tm_fvar x) t) = open_rec k u (subst rho t).
Proof.
  induction t; intros rho x u k Hfv Henv; simpl; auto.
  - (* bvar n *) destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E.
    + simpl. rewrite lookup_cons_eq. reflexivity.
    + simpl. reflexivity.
  - (* fvar y *) 
    assert (Hxy : y <> x) by (intro; subst; apply Hfv; simpl; auto).
    simpl. rewrite lookup_cons_neq by auto. 
    (* RHS: open_rec k u (match lookup y rho with Some s => s | None => fvar y end) — open_rec on a substituted term... *)
```
Hmm, the RHS for fvar y: `open_rec k u (subst rho (tm_fvar y))` = `open_rec k u (match env_lookup y rho with Some s => s | None => tm_fvar y end)`. And the LHS = `match env_lookup y ((x,u)::rho) with Some s => s | None => tm_fvar y end` = `match env_lookup y rho with ... end` (by lookup_cons_neq) = subst rho (fvar y). So we need `subst rho (fvar y) = open_rec k u (subst rho (fvar y))` — is that true? open_rec replaces bvar k with u inside the substituted term (which has no bvar k... well, it could! rho's images could contain bvar k). Hmm! Since rho y might contain a bvar k, open_rec would replace it. That's a problem: the RHS substitutes into the *image*, while the LHS doesn't. Hmm!

Wait, let me redo: LHS = `subst ((x,u)::rho) (open_rec k (fvar x) (fvar y))` = `subst ((x,u)::rho) (fvar y)` (since open_rec on fvar is identity) = `lookup y ((x,u)::rho)` resolved = `lookup y rho` resolved = the image (if any). RHS = `open_rec k u (subst rho (fvar y))` = `open_rec k u (image)` — which applies open to the image, potentially replacing bvars in the image ✗.

So the lemma is FALSE if rho's images contain bvar k! Hmm. Hmm, but wait: is that actually a semantic problem? In our use, the images are locally closed values (from LR → lc ✓), so they contain no free bvars (lc_at 0) → open_rec k u on them at k ≥ 0... hmm, open_rec k u s with s locally closed: does it change s? open_rec k replaces bvar k occurrences; if s is lc_at 0, then no bvar is free... but wait, under binders within s, bvar indices increase; lc_at 0 s means all bvars in s are bound within s. open_rec k u s replaces FREE occurrences of bvar k in s (at depth k). If s is locally closed, there are no free bvars at any depth... hmm: lc_at 0 s means every bvar in s is bound. open_rec k with k ≥ 0: free bvar k at depth k in s — if s is locally closed, every bvar i occurring at depth d has i < d, so i ≠ d... hmm, bvar k at depth k: we need i = k and i < depth — at the occurrence's depth d, i < d. If i = k and d = k, contradiction ✓. But the depth in open_rec is the depth within s starting at k, so the "free" occurrences are exactly those with index equal to the current depth, which are unbound in s → contradiction with locally_closed ✓. So open_rec k u s = s when lc_at 0 s (for any k) ✓.

So the lemma needs the images to be locally closed. Let me add the hypothesis `Henv_lc : forall y s, env_lookup y rho = Some s -> locally_closed s` — but that's env_sem's first component ✓. Hmm, but then the induction gets more complex. Alternatively, restrict the lemma statement:

```coq
Lemma subst_open_rec : forall rho x u k t,
  ~ In x (fv_list t) ->
  ~ In x (env_atoms rho) ->
  (forall y s, env_lookup y rho = Some s -> lc_at 0 s) ->   (* images lc *)
  subst ((x,u)::rho) (open_rec k (tm_fvar x) t) = open_rec k u (subst rho t).
```
Hmm, but in the fvar case, the RHS applies open_rec to the image — and we need `open_rec k u image = image` ✓ from lc ✓. And in the bvar case, and the abs case (IH with k := S k) ✓.

Hmm, hold on. There's another issue with the bvar case in the abs: `open_rec (S k)` vs the subst. Let me redo the abs case: 
- t = tm_abs T body:

Actually, the issue: `induction H` where H : has_type Gamma t T and the goal is `forall rho, env_sem Gamma rho -> LR T (subst rho t)` — Coq's induction on H will need to generalize the goal over Gamma, t, T. Actually, a common pitfall: after `intros ... H. induction H`, Coq may complain "cannot find the elimination combinator" or produce a weak IH if the goal mentions the variables in a non-uniform way. Let me just write it and see.

Part B: LR and closure lemmas.

```coq
(* ---------- logical relation ---------- *)

Fixpoint LR (T : ty) (t : tm) : Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_Bool => locally_closed t /\ SN t
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 =>
      locally_closed t /\ SN t /\ (forall u, LR T1 u -> LR T2 (tm_app t u))
  end.

Lemma LR_lc : forall T t, LR T t -> locally_closed t.
Proof. intros T; induction T; intros t H; simpl in H; tauto. Qed.

Lemma LR_SN : forall T t, LR T t -> SN t.
Proof. intros T; induction T; intros t H; simpl in H; tauto. Qed.
```
Hmm, for the arrow case, H : lc t /\ SN t /\ (...). `tauto` extracting SN t: `destruct H as [H1 [H2 H3]]; exact H2` ✓. tauto should handle it.

```coq
Lemma LR_step_forward : forall T t t', LR T t -> step t t' -> LR T t'.
Proof.
  intros T. induction T; intros t t' H Hst.
  - simpl in *. destruct H as [Hlc Hsn]. split.
    + eapply lc_step; eassumption.
    + eapply SN_step; eassumption.
  - simpl in *. destruct H as [Hlc [Hsn Harr]]. split; [|split].
    + eapply lc_step; eassumption.
    + eapply SN_step; eassumption.
    + intros u Hu. apply IHT2 with (t := tm_app t u).
      * apply Harr; assumption.
      * eapply ST_App1; [exact Hst | apply LR_lc with (T := T1); assumption].
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma SN_step : forall t t', SN t -> step t t' -> SN t'.

  intros t t' Hsn Hst. induction Hsn as [t Hred]. apply Hred. assumption.

Lemma SN_step_backward : forall t t', SN t' -> step t t' -> SN t.
Proof.
  intros t t' Hsn Hst. apply SN_intro. intros t'' Hst''.
  assert (t'' = t') by (eapply step_det; eassumption). subst. assumption.
Qed.
```

Part C: substitution infrastructure.

```coq
Definition env := list (atom * tm).

Fixpoint env_lookup (x : atom) (rho : env) : option tm :=
  match rho with
  | [] => None
  | (y, s) :: rho' => if Nat.eqb x y then Some s else env_lookup x rho'
  end.

Fixpoint substenv (rho : env) (t : tm) : tm :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar n => tm_bvar n
  | tm_fvar x => match env_lookup x rho with Some s => s | None => tm_fvar x end
  | tm_app t1 t2 => tm_app (substenv rho t1) (substenv rho t2)
  | tm_abs T body => tm_abs T (substenv rho body)
  | tm_true => tm_true
  | tm_false => tm_false
  end.

Definition env_atoms (rho : env) : list atom :=
  flat_map (fun p => p.1 :: fv_list (p.2)) rho.
```
Wait, `fv_list` needs to be defined:
```coq
Fixpoint fv_list (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => []
  | tm_fvar x => [x]
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv_list t1 ++ fv_list t2
  | tm_abs _ body => fv_list body
  | tm_true => []
  | tm_false => []
  end.
```

`flat_map` requires the `List` import ✓. The notation `p.1` needs a pair destruct or `fst`. `fun p => fst p :: fv_list (snd p)`. Let me write `fun p => (fst p) :: fv_list (snd p)`.

Fresh:
```coq
Definition fresh (L : list atom) : atom := S (fold_right Nat.max 0 L).

Lemma fresh_gt : forall L x, In x L -> x < fresh L.
Proof.
  intros L. induction L as [| a L IH]; intros x H; [contradiction|].
  simpl in H. destruct H as [-> | H].
  - unfold fresh. simpl. lia.
  - assert (x <= fold_right Nat.max 0 L) by (apply IH; assumption).
    unfold fresh. simpl. lia.
Qed.
```
Hmm, `unfold fresh; simpl` — fresh L = S (fold_right Nat.max 0 (a :: L)) = S (Nat.max a (fold_right Nat.max 0 L)). For the first case: need a < S (Nat.max a rest) ✓ lia. For the second: x ≤ rest ≤ Nat.max a rest < S(...) ✓ lia needs to know rest ≤ Nat.max a rest; `Nat.le_max_r` — lia might do it automatically since Nat.max is a known function? lia doesn't handle max automatically unless it's unfolded. Let me add: `apply Nat.le_max_r` as an assertion, or use `pose proof (Nat.le_max_r a (fold_right Nat.max 0 L))`.

```coq
Lemma fresh_notin : forall L, ~ In (fresh L) L.
Proof.
  intros L H. apply fresh_gt in H. lia.
Qed.
```

Now the key substitution lemma:
```coq
Lemma substenv_open_rec : forall t rho x u k,
  ~ In x (fv_list t) ->
  ~ In x (env_atoms rho) ->
  (forall y s, env_lookup y rho = Some s -> locally_closed s) ->
  substenv ((x, u) :: rho) (open_rec k (tm_fvar x) t) = open_rec k u (substenv rho t).
```
Proof by induction on t with rho, x, u, k generalized. Hmm, the induction is on t; the statement has rho and x varying ✓.

- bvar n: `destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E`.
  + LHS: open_rec k (fvar x) (bvar n) = fvar x; substenv ((x,u)::rho) (fvar x) = u. RHS: open_rec k u (substenv rho (bvar n)) = open_rec k u (bvar n) = u ✓.
  + LHS: bvar n → substenv = bvar n. RHS: open_rec k u (bvar n) = bvar n ✓.
- fvar y: 
  LHS: open_rec k (fvar x) (fvar y) = fvar y; substenv ((x,u)::rho) (fvar y) = match env_lookup y ((x,u)::rho) with ... 
  RHS: open_rec k u (substenv rho (fvar y)) = open_rec k u (match env_lookup y rho with ...).
  Since y ≠ x (from ~In x [y]), env_lookup y ((x,u)::rho) = env_lookup y rho. So both sides are `match env_lookup y rho with Some s => ... | None => fvar y end`, except the RHS applies open_rec k u to it. So destruct (env_lookup y rho):
    * Some s: LHS = s; RHS = open_rec k u s = s (need lc s → open_rec k u s = s ✓ lemma required: `open_rec_lc : forall t k u, locally_closed t -> open_rec k u t = t`).
    * None: LHS = fvar y; RHS = open_rec k u (fvar y) = fvar y ✓.
- app: ✓ by IH.
- abs T body: ✓ by IH with k := S k (fv_list (abs T body) = fv_list body ✓).
- true/false ✓.

So I need `open_rec_lc : forall t, locally_closed t -> forall k u, open_rec k u t = t`. Proof by induction on t (generalizing k): 
- bvar n: lc_at 0 (bvar n) → n < 0 → contradiction ✓.
- fvar ✓, app ✓, abs: body: local closed? lc_at 0 (abs T body) → lc_at 1 body — not locally_closed (0). Hmm! So the induction needs a generalization: `forall t k, lc_at k t -> open_rec k u t = t`? Let's check: for bvar n at level k: lc_at k (bvar n) → n < k → n ≠ k → open_rec k u (bvar n) = bvar n ✓. For abs at level k: lc_at k (abs T body) → lc_at (S k) body → IH at (S k) gives open_rec (S k) u body = body → open_rec k u (abs T body) = abs T body ✓. 
So: `Lemma open_rec_lc : forall t k, lc_at k t -> forall u, open_rec k u t = t.` ✓ 

- Also need the bvar case in `substenv_open_rec`: LHS uses `open_rec k (tm_fvar x)` and the substenv; ✓ as analyzed.

```coq
Lemma lc_substenv : forall t k, lc_at k t -> forall rho, (forall y s, env_lookup y rho = Some s -> locally_closed s) -> lc_at k (substenv rho t).
```

```coq
Definition env_sem (Gamma : context) (rho : env) : Prop :=
  (forall x s, env_lookup x rho = Some s -> locally_closed s) /\
  (forall x T, Gamma x = Some T -> exists s, env_lookup x rho = Some s /\ LR T s).
```

```coq
Lemma env_sem_cons : forall Gamma rho x T u,
  env_sem Gamma rho -> LR T u -> env_sem (update Gamma x T) ((x, u) :: rho).
Proof.
  intros Gamma rho x T u [Hlc Hty] Hu. split.
  - intros y s Hs. simpl in Hs.
    destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst. injection Hs as ->. apply LR_lc with (T := T). assumption.
    + apply Hlc with y. assumption.
  - intros y T' Hy. simpl.
    destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E; subst. 
      unfold update in Hy. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl in Hy. injection Hy as ->.
      exists u. split; [reflexivity | assumption].
    + apply Nat.eqb_neq in E.
      unfold update in Hy. rewrite E in Hy.
      (* Hy : Gamma y = Some T' *) 
      destruct (Hty y T' Hy) as [s [Hs HLR]]. exists s. split; [assumption | assumption].
Qed.
```
Careful: `update Gamma x T y = if Nat.eqb x y then Some T else Gamma y`. So `unfold update in Hy` gives `(if Nat.eqb x y then Some T else Gamma y) = Some T'`. Then destruct (Nat.eqb x y) or rewrite with the appropriate equation. Since E : Nat.eqb y x = true, and Nat.eqb is symmetric, I need to rewrite Nat.eqb x y. Hmm, there's `Nat.eqb_sym : forall x y, Nat.eqb x y = Nat.eqb y x`? Let me check: in PeanoNat, there is `Nat.eqb_sym : forall x y : nat, (x =? y) = (y =? x)` ✓ I believe it exists. Or I can use `apply Nat.eqb_eq in E` to get y = x, subst y, then Nat.eqb x x reduces via Nat.eqb_refl ✓.

Let me restructure: after `destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E`:
- true case: `apply Nat.eqb_eq in E; subst y.` Then `unfold update in Hy; rewrite Nat.eqb_refl in Hy` ✓ (Nat.eqb x x = true ✓ by Nat.eqb_refl).
- false case: `apply Nat.eqb_neq in E : y <> x`. Then `unfold update in Hy`. Need to rewrite Nat.eqb x y = false: from y ≠ x, `Nat.eqb_neq x y` ✓ gives Nat.eqb x y = false. Apply: `assert (Nat.eqb x y = false) by (apply Nat.eqb_neq; auto)`. Then rewrite ✓.

Actually, let me reconsider the `substenv_open_rec` lemma statement once more. I want:


**LR_of_red_nonabs** (rewritten):

```coq
Lemma LR_of_red_nonabs : forall T t,
  locally_closed t ->
  (forall T' body, t = tm_abs T' body -> False) ->
  SN t ->
  (forall t', step t t' -> LR T t') ->
  LR T t.
Proof.
  intros T. induction T; intros t Hlc Hna Hsn Hred.
  - simpl. split; [assumption|].
    apply SN_intro. intros t' Hst. apply LR_SN with (T := Ty_Bool). apply Hred; assumption.
  - simpl. split; [assumption|split].
    + apply SN_intro. intros t' Hst. apply LR_SN with (T := Ty_Arrow T1 T2). apply Hred; assumption.
    + intros v Hv.
      assert (Hgen : forall v, SN v -> LR T1 v -> LR T2 (tm_app t v)).
      { intros v Hsnv. induction Hsnv as [v Hstepv IHv]. intros Hv.
        assert (Hreds : forall t', step (tm_app t v) t' -> LR T2 t').
        { intros t' Hst.
          destruct (step_app_cases t v t' Hst)
            as [[T' [body [vv [Heq [Hlcabs [Hval [Heq2 Heq3]]]]]]]
               | [[t1' [Hst1 [Hlc2 Heq1]]]
                  | [v1 [t2' [Heq [Hval [Hst2 Heq2]]]]]]].
          - (* abs *) exfalso. exact (Hna T' body Heq).
          - (* app1 *) subst. apply Hred in Hst1.
            simpl in Hst1. destruct Hst1 as [_ [_ Harr]]. apply Harr; assumption.
          - (* app2 *) subst. apply IHv; assumption.
            (* need LR T1 t2' : forward closure of Hv *)
        }
        apply IHT2.
        - apply lc_app; [assumption | apply LR_lc with (T := T1); assumption].
        - intros T' body Heq. discriminate Heq.
        - apply SN_intro. intros t' Hst. apply LR_SN with (T := T2). apply Hreds; assumption.
        - exact Hreds.
      }
      apply Hgen. apply LR_SN with (T := T1). assumption. assumption.
```

```coq
Lemma LR_abs_app : forall A B body u,
  locally_closed (tm_abs A body) ->
  (forall u, LR A u -> LR B (open body u)) ->
  LR A u ->
  LR B (tm_app (tm_abs A body) u).
Proof.
  intros A B body u Hlc Hbody.
  assert (Hgen : forall w, SN w -> LR A w -> LR B (tm_app (tm_abs A body) w)).
  { intros w0 Hsnw. induction Hsnw as [w Hstepw IHw]. intros Hw.
    assert (Hreds : forall t', step (tm_app (tm_abs A body) w) t' -> LR B t').
    { intros t' Hst.
      destruct (step_app_cases (tm_abs A body) w t' Hst)
        as [[T' [body' [vv [Heq [Hlcabs [Hval [Heq2 Heq3]]]]]]]
           | [[t1' [Hst1 [Hlc2 Heq1]]]
              | [v1 [t2' [Heq [Hval [Hst2 Heq2]]]]]]].
      - (* ST_AppAbs: tm_abs A body = tm_abs T' body' *)
        injection Heq as HA Hb. subst T' body'. subst vv.
        rewrite Heq3. apply Hbody. assumption.
      - (* ST_App1: abs steps *)
        rewrite Heq1. exfalso. exact (value_no_step (tm_abs A body) (v_abs A body Hlc) t1' Hst1).
      - (* ST_App2 *)
        subst v1. rewrite Heq2. apply IHw.
        + assumption.
        + apply LR_step_forward with (t := w); assumption.
    }
    apply LR_of_red_nonabs with (T := B).
    - apply lc_app; [assumption | apply LR_lc with (T := A); assumption].
    - intros T' b Heq. discriminate Heq.
    - apply SN_intro. intros t' Hst. apply LR_SN with (T := B). apply Hreds; assumption.
    - exact Hreds.
  }
  intros Hu. apply Hgen; [apply LR_SN with (T := A) | ]; assumption.
Qed.
```

第 48 次思考 | 原始 trace 第 151 行
========================================================================

The IH is `forall x, In x L -> x < fresh L` — a *strict* inequality with fresh L, not `x <= fold_right ...`. So my `apply IH` gives `x < fresh L` but I need `x <= fold_right Nat.max 0 L`. I need a stronger statement: `x <= fold_right Nat.max 0 L`. Let me prove the bound directly by a separate lemma:

```coq
Lemma in_le_fold_max : forall L x, In x L -> x <= fold_right Nat.max 0 L.
Proof.
  intros L. induction L as [| a L IH]; intros x H.
  - contradiction.
  - simpl in H. destruct H as [Heq | H].
    + subst a. apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + apply Nat.le_trans with (m := fold_right Nat.max 0 L); [apply IH; assumption | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_gt : forall L x, In x L -> x < fresh L.
Proof.
  intros L x H. unfold fresh. pose proof (in_le_fold_max L x H). lia.
Qed.
```
✓ Cleaner.
